Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the trees structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 156. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 508 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by trees generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 156, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 508. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on trees structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 768. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 344 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the trees space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 768 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 344. QED.
